The third inequality follows similarly using estimate (
4.34
),
where we
use also the interpolation inequality (
3.114
)
to obtain

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0+)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}_{\frac{8R}{9}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0+)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}_{\frac{8R}{9}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})}\lesssim\left(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}}.

∎

Remark 6.2.2
.

We note that inequalities (
6.9
)–(
6.11
) imply of course

\displaystyle{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})+\left({}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}+\sqrt{{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\right)+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L},

(6.12)

\displaystyle{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L},

(6.13)

\displaystyle{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})+\left(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L}.

(6.14)

Following the above proof but now using
equations (
4.37
) and (
4.38
),
we may in fact directly deduce the inequalities (
6.12
)–(
6.14
)
under the relaxed assumptions of Remark
4.8.4
,
where (
4.1
) is however required to be semilinear and we are in the analogue of case (ii). Thus, the analogue
of Proposition
6.2.1
holds in that case where (
6.9
)–(
6.11
)
are replaced by (
6.12
)–(
6.14
).

6.2.2
Global existence in
L
-slabs

The presence of positive powers of
L
in (
6.9
)–(
6.11
)
means that our smallness assumptions must involve negative powers of
L
in order for the
estimates to close.

Proposition 6.2.3
.

Let
k-1\geq k_{\rm loc}
be sufficiently large and let us assume the case (ii) assumptions. Then there exists an

0<\varepsilon_{\rm slab}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm loc}
and a constant
C>0
implicit in the sign
\lesssim
below such that the following is true.

Given arbitrary
L\geq 1
,

\tau_{0}\geq 0
,
0<\varepsilon_{0}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm slab}
and initial data
(\uppsi,\uppsi^{\prime})

on
\Sigma(\tau_{0})
as in Proposition
4.9.1
, satisfying moreover

\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\varepsilon_{0},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1},

(6.15)

then the unique solution of Proposition
4.9.1
obtaining the data can be extended to
a
\psi

defined on the entire spacetime slab
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L)

satisfying the equation (
4.1
)
and
the estimates

\qquad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0},\qquad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1}.

(6.16)

Proof.

Consider the set
\mathfrak{B}\subset(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L]

consisting of all
\tau_{0}+L\geq\tau_{f}\geq\tau_{0}
such that a solution
\psi
of (
4.1
)
obtaining the data exists on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{f})

and such that
the boostrap assumption (
4.23
) with
p=1

and also the additional bootstrap assumption

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\leq\varepsilon L^{-1}

(6.17)

hold in
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1}:=\tau_{f})
,
where

0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{\rm boot}
is a small constant satisfying

1\gg\varepsilon\gg\varepsilon_{\rm slab}.

(6.18)

(The above relation in particular already constrains
\varepsilon_{\rm slab}
to be sufficiently small.)

By the local well posedness statement Proposition
4.9.1
,
it follows
that since
k-1\geq k_{\rm loc}
and
\varepsilon_{0}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm slab}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm loc}
,
we have that
\tau_{0}+\tau_{\rm exist}\in\mathfrak{B}
and thus
\mathfrak{B}\neq\emptyset
, provided that

\varepsilon
satisfies (
6.18
).
Also note that a fortiori, if
\tau_{f}\in\mathfrak{B}
, then
(\tau_{0},\tau_{f}]\in\mathfrak{B}

and thus
\mathfrak{B}
is manifestly a connected subset of
(\tau_{0},\infty)
.

For
\tau_{1}:=\tau_{f}\in\mathcal{B}
, Proposition
6.2.1
applies in
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})
.
From (
6.10
) and (
6.15
) we obtain

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}+\varepsilon^{1/2}\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}},

(6.19)

where we have used the bootstrap assumptions (
4.23
) and (
6.17
).

From (
6.11
) for
k-1
and (
6.15
), we obtain

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1}+\varepsilon^{1/2}(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}L^{-\frac{1}{2}\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}+\varepsilon^{1/2}\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}.

(6.20)

It follows that for
\varepsilon
satisfying (
6.18
), we obtain

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0},\qquad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-\frac{1}{2}\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}

(6.21)

and plugging the second inequality of (
6.21
) into (
6.20
) we obtain

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1}+\varepsilon^{1/2}\varepsilon_{0}L^{-\frac{1}{2}(\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta})^{2}}L^{-\frac{1}{2}\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}.

(6.22)

Now defining

\gamma_{0}:=\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta},

we have

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-\gamma_{0}},

(6.23)

whence, plugging (
6.23
) into (
6.20
)
we improve to

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1}+\varepsilon^{1/2}\varepsilon_{0}L^{-\gamma_{0}(\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta})^{2}}L^{-\frac{1}{2}\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}.

(6.24)

Defining
\gamma_{i}
iteratively by
\gamma_{i}=\gamma_{i-1}\left(\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}\right)^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}
,
then, in view of the restriction (
3.1
), there exists a first
i
such that
\gamma_{i}\geq 1
, whence we obtain

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1}.

(6.25)

We may now already apply our continuation criterion Corollary
4.9.2
, applied with
p=0
and
k-1
,
to assert the existence of an
\epsilon
, independent of
\tau_{1}
, such that now defining
\tau_{1}:=\min\{\tau_{f}+\epsilon,\tau_{0}+L\}
,
the solution
\psi
extends to a smooth solution of (
4.1
)
on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})
, and moreover, from (
4.41
), that

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1}

(6.26)

holds on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})
,
and from (
4.42
), that

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}

(6.27)

holds on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})
.
It follows that (
6.17
) and (
4.23
) (with
p=1
) hold on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})
and thus

\tau_{1}=\min\{\tau_{f}+\epsilon,\tau_{0}+L\}\in\mathfrak{B}
.

Since
\epsilon
is independent of
\tau_{f}
, and in view also of the connectivity of
\mathfrak{B}
,
it follows that
\mathfrak{B}
is a nonempty open and closed subset of
(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L]

and thus
\mathfrak{B}=(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L]
and hence the solution exists in the entire spacetime slab

\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L)
.

The estimates (
6.21
) and (
6.25
) thus hold in the entire spacetime slab.
This gives (
6.16
).
∎

Remark 6.2.4
.

Examining the proof, it is clear that we have only used (
6.12
)–(
6.14
) and not the
full (
6.9
)–(
6.11
)
of Proposition
6.2.1
. Thus, in view of the comments of Remark
6.2.2
,
the proof holds also for the semilinear case under the relaxed assumptions described in Remark
4.8.4
.
In general, examining the proof, we may in fact relax the assumption of the first inequality of (
6.15
) to the assumption

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\varepsilon_{0}L^{\beta}

(6.28)

for a sufficiently small
\beta
,
in which case the first inequality of (
6.16
) is replaced by

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\varepsilon_{0}L^{\beta}.

(6.29)

This will again be useful for the semilinear case.

6.2.3
The pigeonhole argument

The above assumptions on initial data are sufficient for global existence in the slab, but are not sufficient
to iterate. For this we shall need strengthened assumptions.

Proposition 6.2.5
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
6.2.3
,
there exists a constant
C>0
, implicit in the inequalities
\lesssim
below, a parameter
\alpha_{0}\gg 1

and, for all
\alpha\geq\alpha_{0}
, a parameter
\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}(\alpha)

such that for all
0<\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}(\alpha)
the following holds.

Let us assume that in addition to (
6.15
) we have

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}.

(6.30)

Then the solution
\psi
of (
4.1
)
on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L)
given by Proposition
6.2.3

satisfies the additional estimates

\sup_{\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{0}+L}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\leq\alpha\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},

(6.31)

\sup_{\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{0}+L}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau)\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}(1+\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}^{1/4}L^{(-1+\delta)/2}),

(6.32)

\sup_{\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{0}+L}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau)\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}(1+\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}^{1/4}L^{-1/2}),

(6.33)

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1},\qquad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta},\qquad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}.

(6.34)

Moreover,
for all times
\tau^{\prime}
with
L\geq\tau^{\prime}-\tau_{0}\geq L/2
, we have that

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\leq\frac{1}{2}\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1},

(6.35)

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\leq\frac{1}{2}\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta},

(6.36)

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\leq\frac{1}{4}\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}.

(6.37)

Proof.

Note that by the statement of the proposition, we are in particular
also assuming a priori (
6.15
) for
some

\varepsilon_{0}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm slab}
,
since this is included in the assumptions of Proposition
6.2.3
.
In view now of Corollary
4.5.3
, however, for
\alpha_{0}
sufficiently large,
if say
\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}(\alpha)\ll\alpha^{-3}\varepsilon_{\rm slab}
,
it follows from the additional assumptions (
6.30
) that the estimates (
6.15
)
in fact hold with the specific constant
\varepsilon_{0}:=\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{3}
for all
\alpha\geq\alpha_{0}
.

To obtain (
6.34
) we argue as follows.
We note from the proof of Proposition
6.2.3
that we have the following system of inequalities

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}+\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}},

(6.38)

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}+\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}},

(6.39)

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1}+(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}}+\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}},

(6.40)

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta}+\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}},

(6.41)

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}+(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}}+\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}},

(6.42)

where we have used also the initial data assumptions (
6.30
).

For
\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}(\alpha)
sufficiently small, we have
\varepsilon_{0}\ll 1
and thus
we obtain immediately that

\displaystyle{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta},

(6.43)

\displaystyle{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1}+(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}},

\displaystyle{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}+(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}}.

The first two inequalities of (
6.43
) yield the first two inequalities of (
6.34
),
while the third inequality of (
6.43
) yields the fourth inequality of (
6.34
).
On the other hand, from the latter inequality of (
6.43
) we obtain

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}+\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}L^{-\frac{3}{2}\frac{(1-\delta)(1+2\delta)}{1+\delta}}.

(6.44)

Note that
-\frac{3}{2}\frac{(1-\delta)(1+2\delta)}{1+\delta}>-2+\delta
.
We may however improve (
6.44
) iteratively as follows:
If

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-\gamma},

for
\gamma<2-\delta
,
then

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-\gamma}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-\gamma},

hence, plugging this again into the final inequality of (
6.43
) to estimate the nonlinear term,
we obtain

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}+\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}L^{-\frac{3}{2}\frac{(1-\delta)(\gamma+2\delta)}{1+\delta}}.

(6.45)

Setting
\gamma_{0}=\frac{3}{2}\frac{(1-\delta)(1+2\delta)}{1+\delta}
, and defining inductively

\gamma_{i}=\frac{3}{2}\frac{(1-\delta)(\gamma_{i-1}+2\delta)}{1+\delta}
,
we have that there exists a first
i\geq 1
such that
\frac{3}{2}\frac{(1-\delta)(\gamma_{i}+2\delta)}{1+\delta}>2-\delta
.
It follows that (
6.45
) holds for
\gamma=\gamma_{i}
, hence

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}.

This yields the fifth inequality of (
6.34
).
Note that this implies

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-2+\delta}.

(6.46)

Note also that third inequality of (
6.43
) implies

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1+\delta}.

(6.47)

Finally, plugging these bounds into the fourth inequality of (
6.43
) yields the third inequality of (
6.34
),
completing the proof of (
6.34
).

To obtain (
6.32
), we recall (
6.10
) from Proposition
6.2.1
.
This gives, for sufficiently small
\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{0}+L}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})+C\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}+C\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L},

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}+C\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}L^{(-1+\delta)/2}+C\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}L^{(-2+\delta)/2}L^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}(1+\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}^{1/4}L^{(-1+\delta)/2})

where we have used the first inequality of (
6.30
), the first inequality of (
6.43
),
the estimate (
6.47
) and
the estimate (
6.46
) and we have replaced

\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}
by
\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}^{1/4}

in the penultimate line, sacrificing a quarter power to absorb the resulting
\alpha
term and the constant
C
.

To obtain (
6.33
), we now recall (
6.9
) from Proposition
6.2.1
.
This gives, for sufficiently small
\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{0}+L}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})+C\,\left({}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}+\sqrt{{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\right)+C\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}+C\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}L^{(-2+\delta)/2}+C\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{1/2}\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}L^{-1/2}+C\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}L^{(-2+\delta)/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}(1+\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}^{1/4}L^{-1/2}),

where we have used the second inequality of (
6.30
),
the second and third inequalities of (
6.34
),
the estimate (
6.46
) and the first inequality of (
6.44
), and we have replaced

\varepsilon_{0}^{1/2}
by
\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}^{1/4}

in the penultimate line, sacrificing a quarter power to absorb the resulting
\alpha
term and the constant
C
.

To show (
6.35
)–(
6.37
), we first apply the pigeonhole principle as in
[
DR09
]

to
the inequality

\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{0}+L}\left(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}(\tau^{\prime})+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}(\tau^{\prime})+\alpha^{-1}L^{1-\delta}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}(\tau^{\prime})\right)d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},

which, upon addition, follows from the first, second and fourth
inequalities of the estimate (
6.34
) already shown.
Recalling from (
3.93
) that for both
p=2-\delta
and
p=1
, we have

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\gtrsim\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\gtrsim\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{E}}},

we obtain
that there exists
\tau^{\prime\prime}\in[\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L/2]
, whose precise value depends on the solution, such that

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\cdot L^{-1},

(6.48)

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\cdot L^{-1},

(6.49)

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta}\cdot L^{-1}.

(6.50)

Now in view of the interpolation estimate (
3.113
) of Proposition
3.6.12
we have

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\lesssim\left(\,\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)^{1-\delta}\left(\,\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)^{\delta}\leq\left(\,\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\right)^{1-\delta}\left(\sup_{\tau\in[\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L]}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\right)^{\delta}

and thus

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}L^{-1+\delta}

(6.51)

where we have used (
6.49
) and the estimate
for
\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}
contained in (
6.34
).

Now
we apply (
6.10
)
and (
6.11
) again, with
\tau^{\prime\prime}
in place of
\tau_{0}
,
using (
6.48
), (
6.51
) and (
6.50
) to bound the initial data,
to obtain that
for all
\tau_{0}+L\geq\tau^{\prime}\geq\tau_{0}+L/2
, we have

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}L^{-1},

(6.52)

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}L^{-1+\delta},

(6.53)

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})

\displaystyle\sim

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-2+\delta}.

(6.54)

Thus, in view of the requirement
\alpha\geq\alpha_{0}\gg 1
, for sufficiently large
\alpha_{0}
we may
absorb the constants implicit in
\lesssim
by explicit constants of our choice by adding extra positive
\alpha

powers to the right hand side of (
6.53
) and (
6.54
).
In this way, we obtain the specific
estimates (
6.36
) and (
6.37
) for all
\tau^{\prime}\geq\tau^{\prime\prime}
and thus in particular for
all
L\geq\tau^{\prime}-\tau_{0}\geq L/2
. In the same way, we also obtain the specific constant of the estimate
of (
6.31
) which will be convenient in our scheme.
∎

Let us state an alternative “relaxed” version of the above proposition.

Proposition 6.2.6
.

Under the relaxed assumptions for Proposition
6.2.3

described in Remark
6.2.4
,
there exists a constant
C>0
, implicit in the inequalities
\lesssim
below, a parameter
\beta>0
sufficiently small, a parameter
\alpha_{0}\gg 1

and, for all
\alpha\geq\alpha_{0}
, a parameter
\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}(\alpha)

such that the following holds.

Given
0<\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}
,
let us assume in addition that

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}L^{\beta},\quad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}L^{\beta},\quad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{1/2}L^{-1+\beta},\quad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta+\beta},\quad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta+\beta}.

(6.55)

Then the solution
\psi
of (
4.1
)
on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L)
given by Proposition
6.2.3

satisfies the additional estimates

\sup_{\tau\leq\tau\leq\tau_{0}+L}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\leq\alpha^{\beta}\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}L^{\beta},\qquad\sup_{\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{0}+L}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\leq\alpha^{\beta}\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}L^{\beta},

(6.56)

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}L^{\beta},\quad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}L^{\beta},\quad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\beta},\quad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta+\beta},\quad{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta+\beta}.

(6.57)

Moreover,
for all times
\tau^{\prime}
with
L\geq\tau^{\prime}-\tau_{0}\geq L/2
, we have that

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2\beta}L^{-1+\beta},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2\beta}L^{-1+\delta+\beta},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{1+2\beta}L^{-2+\delta+\beta}.

(6.58)

Proof.

The proof is similar to that of Proposition
6.2.5
and is left to the reader.
∎

Remark 6.2.7
.

The above proposition can now immediately be seen to also hold in
the semilinear case, with the relaxed assumptions described in Remark
4.8.4
.

6.2.4
The iteration: proof of Theorem
5.1
in case (ii)

We may now prove Theorem
5.1
in case (ii).

We shall proceed iteratively.

We define
\tau_{0}=1
,
L_{0}=1
,
L_{i}=2^{i}
,
\tau_{i+1}=\tau_{i}+L_{i}
,
and fix
\alpha\geq\alpha_{0}
so that the statement of Proposition
6.2.5
holds.
(Note that we shall no longer track the dependence of constants and parameters on
\alpha
since it is now fixed; one could simply take
\alpha=\alpha_{0}
.)

For
0<\varepsilon_{0}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm global}
and
\varepsilon_{\rm global}
sufficiently small,
since

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\varepsilon_{0},

then in view of Corollary
4.5.3
, we have

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\lesssim\varepsilon_{0},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}.

Thus, for sufficiently small
\varepsilon_{\rm global}\ll\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}
,
it
follows that there exists a
\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}(\varepsilon_{0})\sim\varepsilon_{0}
,
satisfying moreover
2\alpha\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm slab}
,
such that

\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\leq\frac{1}{2}\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}

and

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}.

Finally, with our restriction on the definition of
\varepsilon_{0}
, we may also write

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\alpha\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}.

In general, given

\tau_{i}\geq 1
defined above,
\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}
, and a solution
\psi

of (
4.1
) on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{i})

such that

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{i})\leq\alpha\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}

(6.59)

and

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{i})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{i},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{i})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{i},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{i})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}\alpha L^{-1},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{i})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}\alpha L_{i}^{-1+\delta},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{i})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}\alpha^{2}L_{i}^{-2+\delta},

(6.60)

note that by our restrictions on
\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}
, the assumptions of Proposition
6.2.3

hold with
\alpha^{2}\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}
in place of
\varepsilon_{0}
(here we are using (
6.59
) to invoke Corollary
4.5.3

to rewrite (
6.60
) in terms of the calligraphic energies; cf. the
first lines of the proof of Proposition
6.2.5
) and the assumptions of
Proposition
6.2.5
then apply with
\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}
in place of
\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}
,
where both propositions are understood now with

\tau_{i}
,
\tau_{i+1}
in place of
\tau_{0}
,
\tau_{1}
.
It follows that the solution
\psi
extends to a solution defined also in

\mathcal{R}_{i}:=\mathcal{R}(\tau_{i},\tau_{i}+L_{i})
satisfying the estimates
(
6.31
)–(
6.34
) while for
\tau^{\prime}=\tau_{i+1}=\tau_{i}+L_{i}

we have in addition (
6.36
)–(
6.37
).
We have thus

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{i+1})

\displaystyle\leq\alpha\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\alpha\hat{\varepsilon}_{i+1},

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{i+1})

\displaystyle\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}(1+\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}^{1/4}L_{i}^{-(1+\delta)/2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{i+1}

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{i+1})

\displaystyle\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}(1+\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}^{1/4}L_{i}^{-1/2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{i+1},

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{i+1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}\alpha L_{i}^{-1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{i+1}\alpha L_{i+1}^{-1},

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{i+1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}\alpha L_{i}^{-1+\delta}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{i+1}\alpha L_{i+1}^{-1+\delta},

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{i+1})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{4}\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}\alpha^{2}L_{i}^{-2+\delta}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hat{\varepsilon}_{i+1}\alpha^{2}L_{i+1}^{-2+\delta},

as long as

\hat{\varepsilon}_{i+1}:=\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}(1+\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}^{1/4}L_{i}^{-(1+\delta)/2}).

(6.61)

Now note that for
\varepsilon_{\rm global}
sufficiently small, the above inductive definition (
6.61
)
of
\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}

ensures that
\hat{\varepsilon}_{i}\leq 2\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}
for all
i
.

It follows that a solution exists in
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\infty)=\cup\mathcal{R}(\tau_{i},\tau_{i}+L_{i})

and in each interval the estimates (
6.34
) hold.

We obtain finally that for all
\tau\geq 1
we have

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau)\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}+C\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}^{3/2},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau)\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}+C\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}^{3/2},

(6.62)

\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau}{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\log(\tau+1),

(6.63)

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\tau^{-1+\delta},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}(v,\tau)\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\tau^{-1+\delta},

(6.64)

\int_{\tau}^{\infty}{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\tau^{-1+\delta},

(6.65)

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\tau^{-2+\delta},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}(v,\tau)\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\tau^{-2+\delta},

(6.66)

\int_{\tau}^{\infty}{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu4$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\tau^{-2+\delta}.

(6.67)

Here, by
\mathcal{F}(v,\tau)
we denote the restriction of the flux on
\underline{C}_{v}
to
J^{+}(\Sigma(\tau))
.

Let us note that we may improve, a posteriori, the inequality (
6.63
) to

\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\infty}{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}.

(6.68)

To show (
6.68
), for all
\tau\geq\tau_{0}
,
we reapply the estimates of Proposition
4.8.1
globally on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau)
. To control the
nonlinear error bulk integrals, redecompose the domain in dyadic time slabs and apply the estimates
proven and sum. In view of the estimates,
all these error bulk integrals can be controlled by
C\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}^{3/2}
.

6.2.5
Alternative proof using Proposition
6.2.6
and the semilinear case

We note that we may prove the theorem alternatively using Proposition
6.2.6
.
We first fix
\alpha\geq\alpha_{0}
, and now define
L_{i}:=\alpha^{i}
.
Here our iterative assumption is

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}L_{i}^{\beta},\quad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}L_{i}^{\beta},\quad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L_{i}^{-1+\beta},\quad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L_{i}^{-1+\delta+\beta},\quad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L_{i}^{-2+\delta+\beta}.

In view of (
6.56
) and (
6.58
) and the definition of
L_{i}
, we see that this is closed under iteration.
We obtain thus existence in
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\infty)
, and at first instance the bounds

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\tau^{\beta},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\tau^{\beta},

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\tau^{-1+\beta},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\tau^{-1+\delta+\beta},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\tau^{-2+\delta+\beta}.

These bounds are of course weaker than those of (
6.62
)–(
6.67
).
The
\tau^{\beta}
factors terms may be, however, removed a posteriori, as in the last paragraph of Section
6.2.4
,
by revisiting the estimates
globally on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau)
, controlling the nonlinear error bulk integrals by redecomposing into dyadic time slabs,
applying the estimates already proven, and summing.

Thus this proof in the end yields finally the same estimates as before, but where (
6.62
) is replaced by

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}.

(6.69)

Remark 6.2.8
.

In view of Remark
6.2.7
, this proof in particular
applies to the semilinear case with the relaxed assumptions of Remark
4.8.4
,
giving the result for case (ii) as stated in Remark
5.1
.

Remark 6.2.9
.

The disadvantage of this alternative proof over the proof given in Section
6.2.4
is that to obtain the fundamental
top-order orbital stability statement (
6.69
),
one must revisit the estimates globally. We thus believe that the proof in Section
6.2.4
better reflects the dyadically localised
philosophy of our method.

6.3
Case (iii)

We now turn to the most general case we consider, case (iii), where
we do not assume that (
3.3
) follows from a physical space identity (
3.15
), but only
assume (
3.3
) as a black box, together with the weaker physical space identity described
in Section
3.4.3
.

This case is slightly more involved because we must combine the estimate originating from
Section
3.4.3

with the estimate originating in Section
3.2
.
This alters a bit the numerology of the number of derivatives we must take, but
the basic scheme is the same as case (ii).

6.3.1
The hierarchy of inequalities

Proposition 6.3.1
.

Let
k
be sufficiently large and let us assume the case (iii) assumptions.
There exist constants
C>0
,
c>0
and an
\varepsilon_{\rm boot}>0
sufficiently small
such that the following is true.

Consider a region
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})

and a
\psi
solving (
4.1
) on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})
, satisfying
moreover (
4.23
) for
p=0
and
0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{\rm boot}
.
Let us assume moreover that

\tau_{1}\leq\tau_{0}+L

for some arbitrary
L>0
.
We have the following hierarchy of inequalities:

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{F}}}(v,\tau_{1}),\quad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{1}),\quad c\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})+AC\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+C\,\left({}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}+\sqrt{{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\right)+C\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L}\,,

(6.70)

\displaystyle{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})+{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}+\sqrt{{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}+{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L}\,,

(6.71)

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{F}}}(v,\tau_{1}),\quad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{1}),\quad c\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})+AC\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+C\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}+C\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L}\,,

(6.72)

\displaystyle{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})+C\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}+C\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L}\,,

(6.73)

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{F}}}(v,\tau_{1}),\quad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{1}),\quad c\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})+AC\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+C\left(\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}}

(6.74)

\displaystyle\qquad+C\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L}\,,

\displaystyle{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})+\left(\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}}

(6.75)

\displaystyle\qquad+{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L}.

Proof.

Again we recall that if
\varepsilon_{\rm boot}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm loc}
, then the assumption of Proposition
4.8.1
holds. The proposition then follows from Proposition
4.8.1

as in the proof of Proposition
6.2.1
, where we have used also the crucial estimate

A\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}\accentset{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\xi$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim AC\,{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}},

which follows from
the fundamental relation (
3.100
).
∎

6.3.2
Global existence in
L
-slabs

To show global existence in

L
-slabs, we require a minor modification
of the assumptions of Proposition
6.2.3
.

Proposition 6.3.2
.

Let
k-2\geq k_{\rm loc}
be sufficiently large and let us assume the case (iii) assumptions. Then there exists a positive constant
\varepsilon_{\rm slab}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm loc}
and a constant
C>0
implicit in the sign
\lesssim
below such that the following is true.

Given arbitrary
L\geq 1
,

\tau_{0}\geq 0
,
0<\varepsilon_{0}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm slab}
and initial data
(\uppsi,\uppsi^{\prime})

on
\Sigma(\tau_{0})
as in Proposition
4.9.1
, satisfying moreover

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\varepsilon_{0},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1},

(6.76)

then the unique solution of Proposition
4.9.1
obtaining the data can be extended to
a
\psi

defined on the entire spacetime slab
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L)

satisfying the equation (
4.1
)
and
the estimates

{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0},\qquad{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1}.

(6.77)

Proof.

Consider the set
\mathfrak{B}\subset(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L]

consisting of all
\tau_{0}+L\geq\tau_{f}\geq\tau_{0}
such that a solution
\psi
of (
4.1
)
obtaining the data exists on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{f})

and such that
the boostrap assumption (
4.23
) (with
p=1
)
and also the additional bootstrap assumption

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\leq\varepsilon L^{-1}

(6.78)

hold in
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1}:=\tau_{f})
,
where

0<\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{\rm boot}

is a small constant satisfying

1\gg\varepsilon\gg\varepsilon_{\rm slab}.

(6.79)

(The above relation in particular already constrains
\varepsilon_{\rm slab}
to be sufficiently small.)

By the local well posedness statement Proposition
4.9.1
,
it follows
that since
k-2\geq k_{\rm loc}
and
\varepsilon_{0}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm slab}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm loc}
,
we have that
\tau_{0}+\tau_{\rm exist}\subset\mathfrak{B}
and thus
\mathfrak{B}\neq\emptyset
, provided that

\varepsilon
satisfies (
6.79
).
Also note that a fortiori, if
\tau_{f}\in\mathfrak{B}
, then
(\tau_{0},\tau_{f}]\in\mathfrak{B}

and thus
\mathfrak{B}
is manifestly a connected subset of
(\tau_{0},\infty)
.

Proposition
6.3.1
holds for
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})
with
\tau_{1}:=\tau_{f}
for any
\tau_{f}\in\mathfrak{B}
.
Adding the equations (
6.72
)–(
6.75
) pairwise with a suitable constant
so as to absorb the term multiplying
A
, with
k-2
replacing
k
for the latter pair,
we obtain the
system:

\displaystyle{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})+{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}+{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L},

(6.80)

\displaystyle{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})+\left(\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}}+{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}}\sqrt{L}.

(6.81)

We now obtain that

{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0},\qquad{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1}.

(6.82)

We may now already apply our continuation criterion Corollary
4.9.2
, applied with
p=0
and
k-2
,
to assert the existence of an
\epsilon
, independent of
\tau_{1}
, such that now defining
\tau_{1}:=\min\{\tau_{f}+\epsilon,\tau_{0}+L\}
,
the solution
\psi
extends to a smooth solution of (
4.1
)
on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})
,
and moreover, from (
4.41
), that

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}L^{-1}

(6.83)

holds on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})

and, from (
4.42
), that

{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}

(6.84)

holds on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})
.
It follows that (
6.78
) and (
4.23
) (with
p=1
) hold on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1})
and thus

\tau_{1}=\min\{\tau_{f}+\epsilon,\tau_{0}+L\}\in\mathfrak{B}
.

Since
\epsilon
is independent of
\tau_{f}
, and in view also of the connectivity of
\mathfrak{B}
,
it follows that
\mathfrak{B}
is a nonempty open and closed subset of
(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L]

and thus
\mathfrak{B}=(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L]
and hence the solution exists in the entire spacetime slab

\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L)
.

The estimates (
6.82
) thus hold in the entire spacetime slab.
This gives (
6.77
).
∎

6.3.3
The pigeonhole argument

The above assumptions on initial data are sufficient for global existence in the slab, but are not sufficient
to iterate. For this we shall need strengthened assumptions.

Proposition 6.3.3
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
6.3.2
,
there exists a constant
C>0
, implicit in the inequalities
\lesssim
below,
a parameter
\alpha_{0}\gg 1

and, for all
\alpha\geq\alpha_{0}
, a parameter
\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}(\alpha)

such that for all
0<\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}(\alpha)
the following holds.

Let us assume in addition to (
6.76
) that we have

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu4$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta},\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu6$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau_{0})\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}.

(6.85)

Then the solution
\psi
of (
4.1
)
on
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{0}+L)
given by Proposition
6.2.3

satisfies the additional estimates

\sup_{\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{0}+L}\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau)\leq\alpha\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},

(6.86)

\sup_{\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{0}+L}\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau)\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}(1+\alpha L^{-1/4}),

(6.87)

\sup_{\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{0}+L}\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau)\leq\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}(1+\alpha L^{-1/4}),

(6.88)

{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1},\qquad{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu4$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu5$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta},

(6.89)

{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu6$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu7$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}.

(6.90)

Moreover,
for all times
\tau^{\prime}
with
L\geq\tau^{\prime}-\tau_{0}\geq L/2
, we have that

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\leq\frac{1}{2}\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1},

(6.91)

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu4$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\leq\frac{1}{2}\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta},

(6.92)

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu6$}}}{\mathfrak{E}}}(\tau^{\prime})\leq\frac{1}{4}\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{2}L^{-2+\delta}.

(6.93)

Proof.

The proof follows closely that of Proposition
6.2.5
.

Note again that by the statement of the proposition, we are in particular
also assuming a priori (
6.76
) for
some

\varepsilon_{0}\leq\varepsilon_{\rm slab}
,
since this is included in the assumptions of Proposition
6.3.2
.
In view now of Corollary
4.5.3
, for
\alpha_{0}
sufficiently large,
if say
\hat{\varepsilon}_{\rm slab}(\alpha)\ll\alpha^{-3}\varepsilon_{\rm slab}
,
it follows from the additional assumptions (
6.85
) that
the estimates (
6.76
)
in fact hold with the specific constant
\varepsilon_{0}:=\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha^{3}
for all
\alpha\geq\alpha_{0}
.

We now revisit (
6.70
)–(
6.75
) and add again pairwise.
We now obtain that

{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu1$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(2-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0},\qquad{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu4$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu5$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1+\delta},

(6.94)

{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+{}^{\chi}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu3$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}\lesssim\hat{\varepsilon}_{0}\alpha L^{-1}+\left(\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,{}^{\rho}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k\mkern-6.0mu-\mkern-6.0mu2$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}\right)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}}+(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{1-\delta}{1+\delta}}(\,\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{X}}})^{\frac{2\delta}{1+\delta}}},

(6.95)
