and, thus, the main content appears already around Minkowski space. Accordingly, the proof will be given in detail for the case that
(\mathcal{M},g_{0})
is Minkowski space, for the two model nonlinearities

N^{\mu\nu}(\psi,x)\partial_{\mu}\psi\partial_{\nu}\psi=\partial_{u}\psi\partial_{v}\psi,\qquad N^{\mu\nu}(\psi,x)\partial_{\mu}\psi\partial_{\nu}\psi=\not{g}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{A}\psi\not{\nabla}_{B}\psi,

where
\not{g}=r^{2}\gamma
is the induced round metric and
\not{\nabla}
its associated connection. See Section
C.1
. The more general nonlinearities and the Kerr case are discussed in Section
C.2
.

C.1
Two model nonlinearities on Minkowski space

Throughout this section
(\mathcal{M},g_{0})
denotes Minkowski space (see Section
2.7.1
).
Note that in the region
r\geq\frac{8R}{9}
which will be considered here,
the
r
of (
2.1
) coincides with the standard radial coordinate (
B.1
) used
in Appendix
B
and thus coincides
with what was denoted by as
\tilde{r}
in Section
2.7.1
.

Recall the
(u,v,\theta,\phi)
coordinate system. In the region
r\geq R
the null vector fields take the form

L=\partial_{v},\qquad\underline{L}=\partial_{u}.

The spheres
\Sigma(\tau)\cap\underline{C}_{v}
are round,
\not{g}=r^{2}\gamma
denotes the induced round metric and
\not{\nabla}
its associated connection.
Define

\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}:=\sup_{\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{1}}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}(\tau),\qquad\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}:=\sup_{\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{1}}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}(\tau),\qquad\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}:=\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}(\tau)d\tau,

and similarly for
\ll k
replacing
k
, and with
R
subscripts added, etc. This notation will be used throughout this section.

The main result of this section is the following.

Proposition C.1.1

(Model nonlinearities on Minkowski space)
.

Fix
\delta\leq p\leq 2-\delta
and
k\geq 7
and let

\psi
be a smooth function in
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\cap\{r\geq 8R/9\}

satisfying (
4.29
).

Then the nonlinearities

F=\partial_{u}\psi\partial_{v}\psi\qquad\text{and}\qquad F=\not{g}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{A}\psi\not{\nabla}_{B}\psi

both satisfy

\sum_{|{\bf k}|\leq k}\int_{\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau^{\prime},v)\cap\{r\geq R\}}\Big(|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+\frac{1}{r}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+\frac{1}{r}|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\Big)\left|\mathfrak{D}^{\bf k}F\right|+\int_{\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau^{\prime},v)\cap\{r\geq R\}}(\mathfrak{D}^{\bf k}F)^{2}\\
\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{\frac{8R}{9}}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ }\Big(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ \Big)+\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}}\ \bigg(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}\ \bigg)

and

\sum_{|{\bf k}|\leq k}\int_{\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau^{\prime},v)\cap\{r\geq R\}}\Big(r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|+|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+\frac{1}{r}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+\frac{1}{r}|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\Big)\left|\mathfrak{D}^{\bf k}F\right|\\
+\int_{\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau^{\prime},v)\cap\{r\geq R\}}(\mathfrak{D}^{\bf k}F)^{2}\lesssim\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ }\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ }+\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{\frac{8R}{9}}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ }\Bigg(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\Bigg).

The following properties of Minkowski space will be used in the proof of Proposition
C.1.1
. First, for any
\bf k
and any function
\psi
, for
r\geq R
,

|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}L\psi|\lesssim\sum_{|\widetilde{\bf k}|\leq|\bf k|}|L\mathfrak{D}^{\widetilde{\bf k}}\psi|,\qquad|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\underline{L}\psi|\lesssim\sum_{|\widetilde{\bf k}|\leq|\bf k|}|\underline{L}\mathfrak{D}^{\widetilde{\bf k}}\psi|.

(C.1)

Indeed, (
C.1
) follows that
L=\partial_{v}
,
\underline{L}=\partial_{u}
,
T=N=\partial_{u}+\partial_{v}
, and
[\partial_{u},\Omega_{i}]=[\partial_{v},\Omega_{i}]=0
for
i=1,2,3
. Thus
\partial_{u}
and
\partial_{v}
commute with all components of
\mathfrak{D}
. Second, the fact that, for any
\bf k
and any function
\psi
, for
r\geq R
,

|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}(\not{g}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{A}\psi\not{\nabla}_{B}\psi)|\lesssim\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi||\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|,

(C.2)

will also be used.

In the above and what follows, note that when the volume form is omitted, the
usual spacetime volume form is to be understood, and this
contains in particular an omitted
r^{2}\sin\vartheta
factor.

The proof of Proposition
C.1.1
relies on weighted Sobolev inequalities, which are discussed in Section
C.1.1
. In Section
C.1.2
the proof of Proposition
C.1.1
for the case of the nonlinearity
F=\partial_{u}\psi\partial_{v}\psi
is given, followed by the proof for the nonlinearity
F=\not{g}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{A}\psi\not{\nabla}_{B}\psi
in Section
C.1.3
.

C.1.1
Weighted Sobolev inequalities

The proof of Proposition
C.0.1
relies on certain weighted Sobolev inequalities.

Let
d\omega
denote the unit volume form on
S^{2}

d\omega=\sin\vartheta\,d\vartheta\,d\varphi.

Recall (see Section
2.7.1
) that
r(u,v)=\frac{1}{2}(v-u)
. Define
v(R)=v(R,u)=2R+u
, so that
r(u,v(R,u))=R
, and similarly define
u(R,v)=v-2R
, so that
r(u(R,v),v)=R
.

Recall the region
\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)
. In order to avoid the blow up of constants in Sobolev inequalities, the region near the corner
\{u=\tau_{0}\}\cap\{r=R\}
will typically be considered separately from its complement. Accordingly, define

\mathcal{R}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v):=\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\cap\{r\geq R\},\qquad\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v):=\mathcal{R}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\smallsetminus\{v^{\prime}\leq v(R,\tau_{0}+R)\},

(C.3)

and

\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v):=\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\cap\{r\geq R\}\cap\{v^{\prime}\leq v(R,\tau_{0}+R)\}.

(C.4)

Note that

r\leq\frac{3R}{2}\qquad\text{in}\quad\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v).

(C.5)

Define also

T_{1}=T_{1}(v)=\min\{\tau_{1},u(R,v)\},\qquad\text{and}\qquad T_{1}^{\prime}=T_{1}(v^{\prime}),

(C.6)

and

\Sigma_{R}(\tau,v)=\Sigma(\tau,v)\cap\{r\geq R\}.

Proposition C.1.2

(Sobolev inequality on incoming cones
\underline{C}_{v}
)
.

For any
\tau_{0}\leq u\leq\tau_{1}
,
v(R,\tau_{0}+R)\leq v^{\prime}\leq v
,
(\vartheta,\varphi)\in S^{2}
(i.e. for any
(u,v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)\in\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)
), and any function
f\colon\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\to\mathbb{R}
,

r|f(u,v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)|\lesssim\big(1+(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\sum_{k\leq 1}\Big(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}\big(|\underline{L}^{k}f|^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}|\Omega_{i}\underline{L}^{k}f|^{2}\\
+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}|\Omega_{i}\Omega_{j}\underline{L}^{k}f|^{2}\big)(u^{\prime},v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)r^{2}d\omega du^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Proof.

For simplicity, consider first the case that
v^{\prime}\geq v(R,\tau_{1})
, so that
T_{1}^{\prime}=\tau_{1}
. Suppose first that
u\geq\frac{\tau_{1}+\tau_{2}}{2}
. Let
\phi
be a smooth cut off function such that
\phi(\tau_{1})=0
,
\phi(\tau)=1
for
\tau\geq\frac{\tau_{1}+\tau_{2}}{2}
, and

|\phi^{\prime}(\tau)|\leq\frac{4}{\tau_{2}-\tau_{1}},\quad\text{for }\tau_{1}\leq\tau\leq\frac{\tau_{1}+\tau_{2}}{2}.

Now

\partial_{u}\left(\phi(u)|f(u,v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)|^{2}\right)=\phi^{\prime}(u)|f(u,v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)|^{2}+2\phi(u)f(u,v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)\partial_{u}f(u,v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi).

Integrating from
\tau_{0}
to
u
gives

r^{2}|f(u,v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)|^{2}\lesssim\big(1+(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-1}\big)\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}|f(u^{\prime},v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)|^{2}r^{2}du^{\prime}+\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}|\partial_{u}f(u^{\prime},v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)|^{2}r^{2}du^{\prime}.

The result then follows from the standard Sobolev inequality on
S^{2}
,

\sup_{(\vartheta,\varphi)\in S^{2}}|f(u^{\prime},v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)|\lesssim r^{-1}\Big(\int_{S^{2}}\big(|f|^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}|\Omega_{i}f|^{2}+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}|\Omega_{i}\Omega_{j}f|^{2}\big)(u^{\prime},v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)r^{2}\sin\vartheta d\vartheta d\varphi\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Similarly for
u\leq\frac{\tau_{1}+\tau_{2}}{2}
, and similarly for
v(R,\tau_{0}+R)\leq v^{\prime}\leq v(R,\tau_{1})
.
∎

Proposition
C.1.2
in particular implies that

\sup_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}\sum_{|\mathbf{k}|\leq k-3}\Big(r|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+r|\not{\nabla}(\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|\Big)\lesssim\big(1+(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}},

(C.7)

and, for any
s\geq 0
, and any
\tau_{0}\leq u\leq\tau_{1}
,
v(R,\tau_{0}+R)\leq v^{\prime}\leq v
,
(\vartheta,\varphi)\in S^{2}
,

r^{s}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi(u,v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)|\lesssim\big(1+(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\sum_{|\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|+3}\Big(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{s-2}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi(u^{\prime},v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)|r^{2}d\omega du^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(C.8)

It is in the forms (
C.7
) and (
C.8
) that Proposition
C.1.2
will be used below.

Proposition C.1.3

(Sobolev inequality on
\Sigma(\tau)
)
.

For any
\tau_{0}\leq\tau\leq\tau_{1}
,
v(R,u)\leq v^{\prime}\leq v
,
(\vartheta,\varphi)\in S^{2}
, and any function
f\colon\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\to\mathbb{R}
,

|f(\tau,v^{\prime},\vartheta,\varphi)|\lesssim\sum_{k=0}^{1}\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{R}(\tau,v)}r^{-2}\big(|L^{k}f|^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{3}|\Omega_{i}L^{k}f|^{2}+\sum_{i,j=1}^{3}|\Omega_{i}\Omega_{j}L^{k}f|^{2}\big)r^{2}d\omega dv^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
+\Big(\int_{\Sigma_{\frac{8R}{9}}(\tau,v)}r^{-2}|f|^{2}r^{2}d\omega dv^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Proof.

The proof follows from the fundamental theorem of calculus and the standard Sobolev inequality on
S^{2}
, as in the proof of Proposition
C.1.2
.
∎

Proposition
C.1.3
in particular implies that

\sup_{\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}\sum_{|\mathbf{k}|\leq k-4}|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{\frac{8R}{9}}},

(C.9)

where the region
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}
is defined in (
C.4
). It is in the form (
C.9
) that Proposition
C.1.3
will typically be used.

C.1.2
The proof of Proposition
C.1.1
for the nonlinearity
F=\partial_{u}\psi\partial_{v}\psi

Proposition
C.1.1
for the nonlinearity

F=\partial_{u}\psi\partial_{v}\psi,

follows from the following proposition, whose proof is the subject of the present section.

Proposition C.1.4

(Nonlinear error estimates for
F=\partial_{u}\psi\partial_{v}\psi
on Minkowski space)
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
C.1.1
, for each
|\mathbf{k}|\leq k
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\Bigg(\int_{\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\cap\{r\geq R\}}\Big(|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+\frac{1}{r}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+\frac{1}{r}|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\Big)\cdot|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\partial_{u}\psi|\cdot|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\partial_{v}\psi|\\
+\int_{\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\cap\{r\geq R\}}|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\partial_{u}\psi|^{2}\cdot|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\partial_{v}\psi|^{2}\Bigg)\\
\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ }\Big(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ \Big)+\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{\frac{8R}{9}}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}}\ \bigg(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}\ \bigg),

and

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|\cdot|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\partial_{u}\psi|\cdot|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\partial_{v}\psi|\\
\lesssim\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ }\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ }+\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{\frac{8R}{9}}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ }\Bigg(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\Bigg).

Proof.

The separate terms are estimated individually.
We will use (
4.29
) to replace quartic bounds with cubic ones without further comment.
In view of the fact (
C.1
), the proof follows from the estimates (
C.10
)–(
C.16
) below.

The notation of Section
C.1.1
will be used throughout. Recall, in particular, the regions
\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}
and
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}
defined in (
C.3
) and (
C.4
) respectively, the boundedness property (
C.5
) of
r
in
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}
, and the
T_{1}
and
T_{1}^{\prime}
notation defined in (
C.6
).

The constant in the Sobolev inequality (
C.7
) blows up as
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\to 0
. Accordingly, the cases
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
and
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
are typically considered separately. Similarly, the region
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}
, near the corner
\{u=\tau_{0}\}\cap\{r=R\}
, is treated separately.

Estimate in the corner region
\{v^{\prime}\leq v(R,\tau_{0}+R)\}
:

For any
|\tilde{\mathbf{k}}|\leq k+1
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\tilde{\mathbf{k}}|-1}\int_{\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\mathfrak{D}^{\tilde{\mathbf{k}}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{\frac{8R}{9}}}\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}.

(C.10)

Indeed, consider some
|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\tilde{\mathbf{k}}|-1
. Since
k\geq 7
, it follows that either
|\mathbf{k}_{1}|\leq k-4
, or
|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq k-4
. In the former case,

\int_{\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\mathfrak{D}^{\tilde{\mathbf{k}}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\\
\lesssim\sup_{\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\Big(\int_{\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\mathfrak{D}^{\tilde{\mathbf{k}}}\psi|\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{\frac{8R}{9}}}\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime},

by the Sobolev inequality (
C.9
). Similarly when
|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq k-4
.

It follows, in view of the boundedness property (
C.5
), that the estimates of the Proposition are trivial in the corner region. The remainder of the proof thus concerns the region
\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)
.

Estimate for
\partial_{u}\psi
term:

First note that, for any
|\mathbf{k}|\leq k
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\Big(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\Big)+\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}}\ \bigg(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}\ \bigg).

(C.11)

Indeed, suppose
|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|
. Since
k\geq 7
, it follows that either
|\mathbf{k}_{1}|\leq k-3
or
|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq k-3
. If
|\mathbf{k}_{1}|\leq k-3
, then, for
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\int_{v(R,\tau_{0}+R)}^{v}\sup_{u,\theta}r|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}\big(r^{-1-\delta}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|^{2}+r^{-1+\delta}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\big)r^{2}d\omega dudv^{\prime}\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime},

by the Sobolev inequality on the incoming cones (
C.7
). For
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{v(R,\tau_{0})}^{v}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau_{0}\leq u\leq T_{1}^{\prime}\\
\theta\in S^{2}\end{subarray}}|r\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\left(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-1-\delta}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|r^{2}d\omega du\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\left(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-1+\delta}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|r^{2}d\omega du\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dv^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}}{(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\int_{v(R,\tau_{0})}^{v}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-1-\delta}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|r^{2}d\omega dudv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\left(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}}\int_{v(R,u)}^{v}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-1+\delta}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|r^{2}d\omega dv^{\prime}du\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}}{(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}\ }\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}\left(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}}du\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}\ }\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}},

by the Sobolev inequality on the incoming cones (
C.7
).

If
|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq k-2
, then

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\int_{v(R,\tau_{0}+R)}^{v}\sup_{u,\theta}|r^{\frac{1+\delta}{2}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-\frac{1+\delta}{2}}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi||\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|r^{2}d\omega dudv^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\big(1+(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\sum_{|\tilde{\mathbf{k}}|\leq|\mathbf{k}_{2}|+3}\int_{v(R)}^{v}\Big(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-1+\delta}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\tilde{\mathbf{k}}}\psi|^{2}r^{2}d\omega du\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\Big(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-1-\delta}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|^{2}r^{2}d\omega du\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|^{2}r^{2}d\omega du\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}dv^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\big(1+(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}\ }\sum_{|\tilde{\mathbf{k}}|\leq|\mathbf{k}_{2}|+3}\Big(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}\int_{v(R,u)}^{v}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-1+\delta}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\tilde{\mathbf{k}}}\psi|^{2}r^{2}d\omega dudv^{\prime}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}},

by the Sobolev inequality on the incoming cones (
C.8
). Hence, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
,

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}\ }\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}},

and, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
,

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}\ }\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}.

Estimate for
\partial_{v}\psi
term:

Next, for any
|\mathbf{k}|\leq k
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ }\Big(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ \Big).

(C.12)

Indeed, if
|\mathbf{k}_{1}|\leq k-2
, then

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\sup_{u,v,\theta}|r\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\Big(\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-1}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-1}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\big(1+(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}}\Big(\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-1}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}},

by the Sobolev inequality on the incoming cones (
C.7
). Hence, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
,

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime},

and, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
,

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}}.

If
|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|-2
then one similarly estimates,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\int_{v(R,\tau_{0}+R)}^{v}\sup_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau_{0}\leq u\leq T_{1}^{\prime}\\
\theta\in S^{2}\end{subarray}}|r^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\left(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-1}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|r^{2}d\omega du\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\left(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|r^{2}d\omega du\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dv^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\big(1+(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad\times\sum_{|\tilde{\mathbf{k}}|\leq|\mathbf{k}_{2}|+3}\int_{v(R,\tau_{0}+R)}^{v}\left(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-1-\delta}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\tilde{\mathbf{k}}}\psi|r^{2}d\omega du\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-1+\delta}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|r^{2}d\omega du\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dv^{\prime}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\big(1+(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}}\left(\int_{v(R,\tau_{0}+R)}^{v}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{T_{1}^{\prime}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{-1+\delta}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|r^{2}d\omega dudv^{\prime}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}},

by the Sobolev inequality on the incoming cones (
C.8
). Hence, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
,

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}},

and, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
,

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}.

Estimate for
r^{-1}\not{\nabla}\psi
and
r^{-1}\psi
terms:

For any
|\mathbf{k}|\leq k
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-1}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ }\Big(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ \Big),

(C.13)

and

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-1}|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ }\Big(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ \Big).

(C.14)

Indeed, (
C.13
) and (
C.14
) follow as in the proof of (
C.12
), using now the fact that

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-1-\delta}|r^{-1}\not{\nabla}\psi|^{2}+r^{-1-\delta}|r^{-1}\psi|^{2}\leq\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime},

and

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-1+\delta}|r^{-1}\not{\nabla}\psi|^{2}+r^{-1+\delta}|r^{-1}\psi|^{2}\leq\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime},\qquad\int_{v(R,\tau)}^{v}\int_{S^{2}}\left(|r^{-1}\not{\nabla}\psi|^{2}+|r^{-1}\psi|^{2}\right)r^{2}d\omega dv^{\prime}\leq\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}.

Estimate for quartic term:

For any
|\mathbf{k}|\leq k
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|^{2}\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\lesssim\Big(\ \accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ \Big)\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}+\Big(\ \accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ \Big)\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}.

(C.15)

Indeed, supposing first that
|\mathbf{k}_{1}|\leq k-3
,

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|^{2}\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\\
\lesssim\sup_{u,\theta}\left(r|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\right)^{2}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-2}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\lesssim\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}\big(1+(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-2}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2},

by the Sobolev inequality on the incoming cones (
C.7
). Hence, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
,

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|^{2}\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\lesssim\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime},

and, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
,

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|^{2}\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\lesssim\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}.

Similarly, if
|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq k-3
,

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|^{2}\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\lesssim\int_{v(R,\tau_{0}+R)}^{v}\sup_{u,\theta}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}\int_{S^{2}}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}r^{2}d\omega dudv^{\prime}\\
\lesssim\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}\big(1+(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\sum_{|\widetilde{\textbf{k}}|\leq|\textbf{k}_{2}|+3}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-2}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\widetilde{\textbf{k}}}\psi|^{2},

and so one similarly has

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|^{2}\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\lesssim\Big(\ \accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ \Big)\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}.

Estimate for
r^{p-1}\partial_{v}(r\psi)
term:

Finally, for any
|\mathbf{k}|\leq k
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\\
\lesssim\bigg(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\bigg)\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}+\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ }\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ }.

(C.16)

Indeed, consider first the case
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
. Suppose first that
|\mathbf{k}_{1}|\leq k-3
.
Then, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sup_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}(r|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|)\Big(\int_{\mathcal{R}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-3}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\mathcal{R}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime},

by the Sobolev inequality on the incoming cones (
C.7
). Similarly, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\Big(\int_{\mathcal{R}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-2}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\mathcal{R}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-2}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}.

Suppose now
|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq k-3
. Then, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\int_{v(R,\tau_{0})}^{v}\sup_{u,\theta}\big(r^{\frac{p+1}{2}}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\big)\left(\int_{\tau_{0}}^{\tau_{1}}\int_{S^{2}}r^{p-3}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|^{2}r^{2}d\omega du\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}dv

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\left(\int_{\mathcal{R}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-3}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|^{2}r^{2}d\omega dudv\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{|\widetilde{\textbf{k}}|\leq|\mathbf{k}_{2}|+3}\left(\int_{\mathcal{R}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}}\psi|^{2}r^{2}d\omega dudv\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ }\sqrt{\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ },

using again the Sobolev inequality on the incoming cones (
C.8
). Similarly, if
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|\cdot|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\left(\int_{\mathcal{R}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-2}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\sum_{|\widetilde{\textbf{k}}|\leq|\mathbf{k}_{2}|+3}\left(\int_{\mathcal{R}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-2}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\widetilde{\mathbf{k}}}\psi|^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}.

∎

C.1.3
The proof of Proposition
C.1.1
for the nonlinearity
F=\not{g}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{A}\psi\not{\nabla}_{B}\psi

Consider now the nonlinearity

F=\not{g}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{A}\psi\not{\nabla}_{B}\psi.

In view of the property (
C.2
), the proof of Proposition
C.1.1
follows from the following proposition.

Proposition C.1.5

(Nonlinear error estimates for
F=\not{g}^{AB}\not{\nabla}_{A}\psi\not{\nabla}_{B}\psi
on Minkowski space)
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
C.1.1
, for each
|\mathbf{k}|\leq k
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\Bigg(\int_{\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\cap\{r\geq R\}}\Big(|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+\frac{1}{r}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+\frac{1}{r}|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\Big)\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\\
+\int_{\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\cap\{r\geq R\}}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|^{2}\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\Bigg)\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{\frac{8R}{9}}}\Big(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ \Big)

and

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\mathcal{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)\cap\{r\geq R\}}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{\frac{8R}{9}}}\Big(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{R}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}\ \Big).

Proof.

The separate terms are estimated individually, and the proof follows from the estimates (
C.17
)–(
C.21
) below.

The notation of Section
C.1.1
will again be used throughout. As in the proof of Proposition
C.1.4
, the cases
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
and
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
are typically considered separately. Similarly, the region near the corner
\{u=\tau_{0}\}\cap\{r=R\}
is treated separately.

Estimate in the corner region
\{v^{\prime}\leq v(R,\tau_{0}+R)\}
:

For any
|\tilde{\mathbf{k}}|\leq k+1
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\tilde{\mathbf{k}}|-1}\int_{\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Corner}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\mathfrak{D}^{\tilde{\mathbf{k}}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}_{\frac{8R}{9}}}\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}_{R}^{\prime}.

(C.17)

Indeed, (
C.17
) follows exactly as in the proof of Proposition
C.1.4
, using the Sobolev inequality (
C.9
).

The remainder of the proof concerns the region
\check{\mathcal{R}}_{R}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)
.

Estimate for
\partial_{u}\psi
term:

First note that, for any
|\mathbf{k}|\leq k
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}.

(C.18)

Indeed, for
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
, assuming without loss of generality that
|\mathbf{k}_{1}|\leq k-3
,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\sup_{u,v,\theta}r|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\Big(\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-1-\delta}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-1+\delta}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime},

by the Sobolev inequality on the incoming cones (
C.7
), since
p\geq\delta
. For
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
,

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{u}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}.

Estimates for
\partial_{v}\psi
,
r^{-1}\not{\nabla}\psi
, and
r^{-1}\psi
terms:

Next, for any
|\mathbf{k}|\leq k
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}\big(|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+r^{-1}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|+r^{-1}|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\big)\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\Big(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ \Big).

(C.19)

Indeed, for
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
, assuming again without loss of generality that
|\mathbf{k}_{1}|\leq k-3
,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\sup_{u,v,\theta}r|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\Big(\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-1}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{-1}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime},

and similarly, for
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\partial_{v}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\ \accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*},

using again the Sobolev inequality on the incoming cones (
C.7
). Similarly for the
r^{-1}\psi
and
r^{-1}\not{\nabla}\psi
terms, using the fact that

\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}\big(r^{-1}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|^{2}+r^{-3}|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi|^{2}\big)\leq\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(\delta-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime},\qquad\int_{v(R,u)}^{v}\int_{S^{2}}\left(|r^{-1}\not{\nabla}\psi|^{2}+|r^{-1}\psi|^{2}\right)r^{2}d\omega dv^{\prime}\leq\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}.

Estimate for quartic term:

For any
|\mathbf{k}|\leq k
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|^{2}\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\lesssim\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}\Big(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(-1-\delta)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}\ \Big),

(C.20)

which follows as an easy consequence of the Sobolev inequality (
C.7
).

Estimate for
r^{p-1}\partial_{v}(r\psi)
term:

Finally, for any
|\mathbf{k}|\leq k
,

\sum_{|\mathbf{k}_{1}|+|\mathbf{k}_{2}|\leq|\mathbf{k}|}\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\Big(\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}+\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}\ \Big).

(C.21)

Assume again, without loss of generality, that
|\mathbf{k}_{1}|\leq k-3
. Then, using (
C.7
), for
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim\sup_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\Big(\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-3}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-1}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\int^{*}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p-1)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}^{\prime}.

Similarly, for
\tau_{1}-\tau_{0}<1
,

\displaystyle\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-1}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{1}}\psi|\cdot|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\lesssim(\tau_{1}-\tau_{0})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\Big(\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-2}|\partial_{v}(r\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\psi)|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\Big(\int_{\check{\mathcal{R}}(\tau_{0},\tau_{1},v)}r^{p-2}|\not{\nabla}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}_{2}}\psi|^{2}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2}}\lesssim\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$\ll\mkern-6.0muk$}}}{\mathcal{F}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(p)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}\sqrt{\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\underaccent{\scalebox{.8}{\mbox{\tiny$k$}}}{\mathcal{E}}}{}^{*}}.

∎

C.2
More general nonlinearities and Kerr

For more general nonlinearities on Minkowski space of the form

F=N^{\mu\nu}(\psi,x)\partial_{\mu}\psi\partial_{\nu}\psi,

with
N
satisfying a null condition of the form

\sup_{|\xi|\leq 1,r\geq R}\sum_{|\textbf{k}|+s\leq k}\sum_{A,B=1,2}\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\partial_{\xi}^{s}\big(rN^{uu}+N^{uv}+N^{vv}+rN^{Au}+rN^{Av}+r^{2}N^{AB}\big)(\xi,x)\lesssim D_{k},

(C.22)

where the
A
,
B
indices refer to coordinates
\vartheta,\varphi
(recall the
(u,v,\vartheta,\varphi)
coordinate system of Section
2.7.1
), and
D_{k}
are
arbitrary constants, the proof of
Proposition
C.0.1
follows exactly as above, where the bound (
4.29
) is now also used
to obtain pointwise bounds on
\psi
through an easy weighted Sobolev estimate which allow as to invoke (
C.22
).

Remark C.2.1
.

Note that, besides the nonlinearities of the classical null condition
[
Kla86
]
,
our class (
C.22
) includes for instance also nonlinearities which for large
r
take
the form
F=(\sin r)\partial_{u}\psi\partial_{v}\psi
. It does not, however, include even more
general examples like
F=(\sin x)\partial_{u}\psi\partial_{v}\psi
considered recently in
[
AZ22
]
, due to the presence of
the weighted vector fields
\Omega_{i}
in our set of
commutation vector fields
\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}
.

More generally, if
g_{0}
is a metric with asymptotics suitably close to those of Minkowski space, with extra terms suitably small, then the above proof again applies.

We will consider explicitly the case that
g_{0}
is the Kerr metric.
Define double null
(u,v,\theta^{1},\theta^{2})
coordinates on Kerr, when
0<|a|<M
, in terms of the Boyer–Lindquist coordinates
(t,r,\vartheta,\phi)
of
Appendix
A.1
, by

u=t-r_{*},\qquad v=t+r_{*},

with
\theta^{1}
defined implicitly by the relation

F(\theta^{1},r,\vartheta)=0,\quad\text{where}\quad F(\theta^{1},r,\vartheta)=\int_{\theta^{1}}^{\vartheta}\frac{1}{a\sqrt{\sin^{2}\theta^{1}-\sin^{2}\theta^{\prime}}}d\theta^{\prime}+\int_{r}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sqrt{((r^{\prime})^{2}+a^{2})^{2}-a^{2}\sin^{2}\theta^{1}\Delta^{\prime}}}dr^{\prime},

(C.23)

and
\theta^{2}
defined by

\theta^{2}=\phi+h(r_{*},\theta^{1}),\quad\text{where $h$ satisfies}\quad\partial_{r_{*}}h(r_{*},\theta^{1})=\frac{2Mar}{\Sigma\mathrm{R}^{2}},\quad\lim_{r_{*}\to\infty}h(r_{*},\theta^{1})=0.

See for instance
[
PI98
,
DL17
]
.
Here

r_{*}(r,\vartheta)=\int\frac{r^{2}+a^{2}}{\Delta}dr+\int_{r}^{\infty}\frac{(r^{\prime})^{2}+a^{2}-\sqrt{((r^{\prime})^{2}+a^{2})^{2}-a^{2}\sin^{2}\theta^{1}\Delta^{\prime}}}{\Delta^{\prime}}dr^{\prime}+\int^{\vartheta}_{\theta^{1}}a\sqrt{\sin^{2}\theta^{1}-\sin^{2}\theta^{\prime}}d\theta^{\prime},

(C.24)

(note that the expression (
C.24
) is independent of
\theta^{1}
in view of (
C.23
)), where
\int\frac{r^{2}+a^{2}}{\Delta}dr
is a function satisfying

\partial_{r}\int\frac{r^{2}+a^{2}}{\Delta}dr=\frac{r^{2}+a^{2}}{\Delta},

and

\Delta=r^{2}+a^{2}-2Mr,\qquad\Delta^{\prime}=(r^{\prime})^{2}+a^{2}-2Mr^{\prime},\qquad\Sigma=r^{2}+a^{2}\cos^{2}\vartheta,\qquad\mathrm{R}^{2}=r^{2}+a^{2}+\frac{2Ma^{2}r\sin^{2}\vartheta}{\Sigma}.

Note in particular that
r_{*}
is distinct from
r^{\star}
defined in Section
A.1
.

In these double null coordinates the Kerr metric takes the form

g_{a,M}=-\Omega^{2}dudv+\not{g}_{AB}(d\theta^{A}-b^{A}dv)(d\theta^{B}-b^{B}dv),

where

\Omega^{2}=\frac{\Delta}{\mathrm{R}^{2}},\qquad b^{1}=0,\qquad b^{2}=\frac{4Mar}{\Sigma\mathrm{R}^{2}},

and

\not{g}_{11}=\frac{a^{2}(\partial_{\theta^{1}}F)^{2}(\sin^{2}\theta^{1}-\sin^{2}\vartheta)((r^{2}+a^{2})^{2}-a^{2}\sin^{2}\theta^{1}\Delta)}{\mathrm{R}^{2}}+(\partial_{\theta^{1}}h)^{2}\mathrm{R}^{2}\sin^{2}\vartheta,

\not{g}_{22}=\mathrm{R}^{2}\sin^{2}\vartheta,\qquad\not{g}_{12}=\not{g}_{21}=-\mathrm{R}^{2}\sin^{2}\vartheta\partial_{\theta^{1}}h.

In particular, one has

L=\partial_{v}+b^{A}\partial_{\theta^{A}},\qquad\underline{L}=\partial_{u}.

Moreover
T=\partial_{u}+\partial_{v}
and
\Omega_{i}={\Omega_{i}}(\theta^{1},\theta^{2})^{A}\partial_{\theta^{A}}
for
i=1,2,3
, for functions
{\Omega_{i}}(\theta^{1},\theta^{2})^{A}
, so that, in particular,

[\partial_{u},T]=[\partial_{u},\Omega_{i}]=[\partial_{v},T]=[\partial_{v},\Omega_{i}]=0.

The function
b^{2}=b^{2}(u,v,\theta^{1},\theta^{2})
satisfies, for any
k\geq 0
,

\sum_{k_{1}+k_{2}+|\textbf{k}_{3}|\leq k}|\partial_{u}^{k_{1}}(r\partial_{v})^{k_{2}}\Omega^{\textbf{k}_{3}}b^{2}|\leq\frac{C_{k}}{r^{3}},

for large
r
, and hence the commutation relations

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}L\psi|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|\widetilde{\bf k}|\leq|\bf k|}|L\mathfrak{D}^{\widetilde{\bf k}}\psi|+\frac{1}{r^{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{|\widetilde{\bf k}|\leq|\bf k|-1}|\Omega_{i}\mathfrak{D}^{\widetilde{\bf k}}\psi|,

(C.25)

\displaystyle|\mathfrak{D}^{\mathbf{k}}\underline{L}\psi|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|\widetilde{\bf k}|\leq|\bf k|}|\underline{L}\mathfrak{D}^{\widetilde{\bf k}}\psi|+\frac{1}{r^{3}}\sum_{i=1}^{3}\sum_{|\widetilde{\bf k}|\leq|\bf k|-1}|\Omega_{i}\mathfrak{D}^{\widetilde{\bf k}}\psi|,

(C.26)

hold for large
r
. Moreover (
C.2
) remains true. The proof of Proposition
C.0.1
in this case then follows just as in
the case where
g_{0}
was the Minkowski metric, using now (
C.25
) and (
C.26
) in place of (
C.1
).

We note finally that the condition (
C.22
) applied to Kerr indeed includes in particular the nonlinearities considered in
[
Luk13
]
.

Appendix D
The inhomogeneous estimate (
3.3
) on Kerr
in the full subextremal case
|a|<M

The main theorem of
[
DRSR16
]
only states (
3.3
) for the homogeneous case, i.e. the case
F=0
.
In this section, we explicitly address the issue of
the inhomogeneous estimate (
3.3
). We have the following:

Theorem D.1
.

The inhomogeneous estimate (
3.3
) holds on Kerr
in the full subextremal case
|a|<M
.

Though the inhomogeneous estimate (
3.3
) for general
F
, precisely as stated,
indeed can be shown to hold in the full subextremal case
|a|<M
,
it is a little bit delicate
to produce the physical space expression corresponding to the middle term
on the right hand side of (
3.3
), except in the case
|a|\ll M
.
(This difficulty is entirely due to the presence of the term
\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)
on the left hand side.)
The weaker version of the estimate in Remark
3.2
, on the other hand,
which is all that is actually used here,
can essentially be immediately read off from the proof of the main
result of
[
DRSR16
]
.
Since we have no real use for the stronger statement, we prefer here to
give the details of how to directly obtain
the weaker statement (though in the case
|a|\ll M
, we note that
our argument indeed reviews the stronger statement).

Proof (of the weaker version of Remark
3.2.1
).

We will obtain the estimate in three steps.
The reader should refer to
[
DRSR16
]
to follow along.

Step 1.

We first obtain (
3.3
) (in its original form) for general
F
,
but without the
future boundary terms on the left hand side, and where
the regions are all restricted to
r\geq r_{+}
.

For this, note that one can assume without loss of generality that
F
is compactly supported
in spacetime, and thus
it is clearly sufficient to prove Proposition 9.1.1 of
[
DRSR16
]
now for solutions of (
1.2
), with the extra inhomogeneous
terms of (
3.3
) now on the right hand side.

We note that the cutoffs used in the proof of Proposition 9.1.1 already gave rise
to inhomogeneous
terms

\mathcal{T}:=\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}\sum_{m\ell}\left(\int_{-\infty}^{\infty}H\cdot(f,h,y,\chi)\cdot(u,u^{\prime})\right)d\omega\,dr^{*}

(D.1)

(see e.g. (165) of
[
DRSR16
]
).
An inhomogeneity
F

on the right hand side of (
1.2
) contributes
an additional term of the form (
D.1
) in the proof of this proposition,
where
H
is replaced by the Carter separated coefficients of
F
according to formula (43)
of
[
DRSR16
]
. Let us denote this term as
\mathcal{T}_{F}
.
One easily sees, however, that, in view of the inclusion of our inhomogeneity
F
,
the problem can be reduced to the case where one has trivial initial data,
in which case one may work directly with
\psi

without applying a cutoff. Thus we may assume now that we
only
have
\mathcal{T}_{F}
on the right hand side, and
H
below will denote the Carter separated coefficients of
F
. We must now
essentially repeat the steps of the proof of the proposition in order
to produce the desired right hand side.

For this, we first immediately partition
\mathcal{T}_{F}:=\mathcal{T}_{F}^{1}+\mathcal{T}_{F}^{2}+\mathcal{T}_{F}^{3}
, where the summands correspond to the integral of (
D.1
) over the region
R_{-}\leq r\leq R_{+}
(i.e.
R_{-}^{*}\leq r^{*}\leq R_{+}^{*}
, etc.),

\{r\leq R_{-}\}\cup\{R_{+}\leq r\leq R\}
and
r\geq R
, respectively,
where
R_{\pm}
,
R_{\infty}
are as in
[
DRSR16
]

and we require

R\geq R_{\infty}
.

Considering
\mathcal{T}^{1}_{F}
, we further partition

\mathcal{T}^{1}_{F}=\mathcal{T}^{1}_{F,\omega}+\mathcal{T}^{1}_{F,\not{\omega}}

where
\mathcal{T}^{1}_{F,\omega}
is the contribution of the
currents in
H\cdot(f,h,y,\chi)\cdot(u,u^{\prime})
that multiply
\omega
,
and
\mathcal{T}^{1}_{F,\not{\omega}}
denotes the rest.
We note (see formula (102) of
[
DRSR16
]
) that the coefficients of the currents in the sum defining

\mathcal{T}^{1}_{F,\omega}

are then frequency independent for all “trapped” frequencies

(\omega,m,\ell)\in\mathcal{G}_{\natural}
(the expression is simply
E\omega{\rm Im}(H\bar{u})
).
Thus, by adding a suitable compensating term
\mathcal{T}^{1}_{\rm comp}

supported
only in the untrapped frequencies, then

\mathcal{T}^{1}_{F,\omega}+\mathcal{T}^{1}_{\rm comp}

is a sum and integral over
the precise expression
E\omega{\rm Im}(H\bar{u})
,
and thus this integral and
sum may be rewritten via the Plancherel formulae on page 820 of
[
DRSR16
]

as a spacetime integral precisely
like the middle term of (
3.3
), restricted to
r\leq R
, with
V_{0}=\partial_{t}
.

For the remaining terms
\mathcal{T}^{1}_{F,\not{\omega}}-\mathcal{T}^{1}_{\rm comp}
,
note that these are supported entirely in the
non-trapped
frequencies, i.e. in the complement frequency set
\mathcal{G}_{\natural}^{c}
.
Recall now that in estimate (165), we in fact manifestly control a stronger left hand side than
what is written, namely we may substitute
\zeta
with
(1-\frac{r_{\rm trap}}{r})^{2}

(which was the original form of the expression before (163) of
[
DRSR16
]
was invoked).
In particular, since
r_{\rm trap}=0
for all non-trapped frequencies,
we control all derivatives without degeneration
for frequencies in
\mathcal{G}_{\natural}^{c}
.
One may
apply Cauchy–Schwarz to the expression
H\cdot(f,h,y,\chi)\cdot(u,u^{\prime})

and to the integrand summands of
\mathcal{T}^{1}_{\rm comp}
, absorbing the
resulting terms proportional to
|u|^{2}
, together with any frequency coefficients
\omega^{2}
and
\Lambda
, and proportional to
|u^{\prime}|^{2}
, into the bulk controlled
in view of our nondegenerate bulk, at the expense of an additional
term
|H|^{2}
on the right hand side. Upon application of the Plancherel formulae of
page 820, this
produces a bulk term
\int F^{2}
of the form already present on the right hand side of (
3.3
). The term
\mathcal{T}^{2}_{F}
may be treated exactly in the
same way, as here the controlled bulk term is nondegenerate for all frequencies, since the integral
is supported away from trapping.

This leaves the term
\mathcal{T}^{3}_{F}
.
One notes that for
r\geq R
, the coefficients of the currents appearing in (
D.1
)
are frequency independent

up to multipliers which decay exponentially in
r
. Again, by adding and subtracting a compensating term as above, one may thus estimate
the additional term here by the middle
term on the right hand side of (
3.10
) (now restricted to
r\geq R
),
again with a suitable vector field
V_{0}
, after applying
Plancherel, up to an additional term exponentially decaying term,
which may be estimated by
Cauchy–Schwarz, absorbing the term containing derivatives of
\psi
into the bulk on the left hand side just as above, and producing
an extra bulk term
\int F^{2}
of the form already present on the right hand side of (
3.3
).

Finally, we note that to complete the proof
of Proposition 9.1.1 in the inhomogeneous case, it remains to apply the analogue of
Proposition 9.7.1 (this is where the quantitative version of mode stability
[
SR15
]
is appealed to),
which also however produces an additional inhomogeneous term
when applied to (
1.2
).
Examining the original
[
SR15
]
, in particular the proof of Lemma 3.3 of that paper, one sees that
this extra term may be bounded by the
\int F^{2}
term on the right hand side of (
3.3
). This completes the proof of Step 1.

Step 2.

We now apply a sufficiently small multiple of the red-shift multiplier and of a cutoff version of the
J_{\mu}^{T}
-current to obtain
the terms
\sup_{v:\tau\leq\tau(v)}\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\mathcal{F}}(v,\tau_{0},\tau)+\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\mathcal{E}}_{\mathcal{S}}(\tau)
on the left hand side of (
3.3
), and the part of the spacetime integral supported in
r_{0}\leq r\leq r_{+}
, exploiting that one may always absorb the resulting error terms in the bulk already controlled in Step 1. We add these identities to the estimate
obtained above.
As these are physical space identities, the inhomogeneity
F
manifestly generates a term of the form of the middle term in (
3.3
) which may be combined
with the previous such terms to define a new vector field
V_{0}
.
One obtains in this way also a bound on the restriction of the integral defining
\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)
to
\{r\leq R_{-}\}\cup\{r\geq R_{+}\}
.

Note that in the case of very slow rotation
|a|\ll M
, one may chose the
support of the gradient of the cutoff applied to
J_{\mu}^{T}
in the identity above to in fact be in the region
r\leq R_{-}
,
whereas
J_{\mu}^{T}
is moreover coercive in the region
r\geq R_{-}
. Thus, the above argument in fact
yields control of the full
\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)
. This would then complete the proof.
In this case, note that we thus obtain the estimate (
3.3
) in its original from.

Step 3.

In the general subextremal case, however,
J^{T}_{\mu}
is not everywhere coercive in the region

R_{-}\leq r\leq R_{+}
, and
it remains to control the contribution of the finite region
R_{-}\leq r\leq R_{+}
to the energy integral of
the missing term
\accentset{\scalebox{.6}{\mbox{\tiny$(0)$}}}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)
from the left hand side of (
3.3
).

To obtain
the missing part of the energy flux we must thus repeat the proof of Proposition 13.1 of
[
DRSR16
]
,
allowing now an inhomogeneous term
F
. Recall that in this proof one considered a
cutoff version
\tilde{\psi}
, decomposed
\tilde{\psi}=\tilde{\psi}_{1}+\ldots+\tilde{\psi}_{N}
orthogonally
using Carter’s separation, for some large
N
, and
applying a distinct current
J^{V_{i}}_{\mu}
to each summand
\psi_{i}
.

Due to the presence of cutoffs in the proof, one can again read off
the additional terms arising from the new inhomogeneity.
Since outside
the region
R_{-}\leq r\leq R_{+}
,,
all errors from can be absorbed as in the previous steps, while the cutoffs vanish inside the region
R_{-}\leq r\leq R_{+}
, all inhomogeneous
terms in
R_{-}\leq r\leq R_{+}
now arise from
F
, and we may use the notation
\tilde{F}_{i}

of the proof of Proposition 13.1 to denote precisely these new terms. The resulting inhomogeneous
terms that must be controlled are then
\sum_{i}\int_{R_{-}\leq r\leq R_{+}}V_{i}\tilde{\psi}_{i}\cdot\tilde{F}_{i}
.
By orthogonality, we may indeed manifestly
bound this term by the expression in the first term of (
3.10
).

Since all terms of the form of the middle term on the right hand side of (
3.3
)
supported in
r\leq R
(which we generated in the previous two steps) can
of course manifestly be bounded by the first term of (
3.10
),
we obtain finally that (
3.3
) indeed holds with (
3.10
)
replacing the middle term on the right hand side.
∎

We note that obtaining (
3.3
) in the
|a|<M
case
with its middle term in its original form
requires a slightly more delicate
decomposition of
\tilde{\psi}
than the one used in Proposition 13.1 of
[
DRSR16
]
.
Again, since this is not used, we spare the reader the
details of this argument.
